Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in matrix theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 63 and the invariant I(A,B) = 584 mod 3.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by matrix generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 63, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 584. QED.

Theorem 214.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For any matrix module M satisfying the structural decomposition hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 128. The projective resolution has length at most 904 with Betti numbers bounded by 5.

Proof: By the structural decomposition principle applied to matrix theory, we decompose the problem into 128 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 904. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(5/n). QED.
